TL;DR: 本研究比较了逻辑回归与BiLSTM在推特情感分析中的表现，发现传统机器学习方法在中等规模数据上优于深度学习模型，并将模型部署为公开的Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于社交媒体数据快速增长，亟需实时自动化分析公众情绪的系统。

Method: 采用逻辑回归（TF-IDF特征）与BiLSTM模型，在Sentiment140数据集的1万条推文子集上进行对比实验。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率为73.5%，优于BiLSTM的69.17%，且深度学习模型出现轻微过拟合。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模的非正式文本数据，结合强特征提取的传统机器学习方法可优于复杂的深度学习方法。

Abstract: The exponential growth of social media has created an urgent need for automated systems to analyze unstructured public sentiment in real time. This study compares a traditional Logistic Regression model using TF-IDF features with a deep learning Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture on a 10,000-tweet subset of the Sentiment140 dataset. Experimental results show that Logistic Regression outperformed BiLSTM, achieving an accuracy of 73.5% compared with 69.17%, while the deep learning model exhibited mild overfitting. These findings suggest that for medium-scale informal text data, classical machine learning with robust feature extraction can outperform more complex deep learning approaches. Finally, the trained models were integrated into an interactive web application using Streamlit and deployed on Hugging Face Spaces for public access.

</details>


### [114] [Rethinking Local Learning: A Cheaper and Faster Recipe for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04913)
*Hengyu Shi,Tianyang Han,Peizhe Wang,Zhiling Wang,Xu Yang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LoPT的局部学习后训练方法，通过在Transformer模型中间设置梯度边界，降低后训练过程中的内存消耗和计算开销，同时有效保留预训练表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LLM后训练需要在整个模型深度上传播梯度，导致高内存占用和对预训练表示的过度干扰，尤其是在任务监督信号远窄于预训练的情况下，这种全深度耦合显得过于昂贵且侵入性强。

Method: LoPT在Transformer的中间层设置一个梯度边界：后半部分模块通过任务目标进行学习，前半部分则通过轻量级的特征重建目标进行更新，以保持有用表示和接口兼容性，从而限制任务梯度对早期层的直接影响。

Result: 实验表明，LoPT在多种任务上实现了与全模型微调相媲美的性能，同时显著降低了内存消耗、提高了训练效率，并更好地保留了预训练模型的能力。

Conclusion: LoPT通过显式控制梯度传播范围，提供了一种更高效、更轻量的LLM后训练范式，能够在减少资源消耗的同时维持模型性能和预训练知识的完整性。

Abstract: LLM post-training typically propagates task gradients through the full depth of the model. Although this end-to-end structure is simple and general, it couples task adaptation to full-depth activation storage, long-range backward dependencies and direct task-gradient access to pretrained representations. We argue that this full-depth backward coupling can be unnecessarily expensive and intrusive, particularly when post-training supervision is much narrower than pre-training. To this end, we propose \textbf{LoPT}: Local-Learning Post-Training, a simple post-training strategy that makes gradient reach an explicit design choice. LoPT places a single gradient boundary at the transformer midpoint: the second-half block learns from the task objective, while the first-half block is updated by a lightweight feature-reconstruction objective to preserve useful representations and maintain interface compatibility. LoPT shortens the task-induced backward path while limiting direct interference from narrow task gradients on early-layer representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoPT achieves competitive performance with lower memory cost, higher training efficiency and better retention of pretrained capabilities. Our code is available at: https://github.com/HumyuShi/LoPT

</details>


### [115] [Unintended Negative Impacts of Promotional Language in Patent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04926)
*Bingkun Zhao,Chenwei Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了270万份美国专利申请，发现夸张语言使用频率越高，专利获批、转让和上诉成功的可能性越低，尽管这种语言实际上反映了技术的组合新颖性和未来影响力，揭示了专利审查中的性别与经验偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究夸张语言在技术发明中的作用及其对专利审查结果的影响，弥补科学传播与技术创新间语言使用研究的空白。

Method: 使用包含135个经验证的夸张词汇的词典，对270万份美国专利商标局（USPTO）专利申请进行大规模文本分析，并控制多种混杂因素，分析其与专利授予、转让和上诉结果的关系。

Result: 1. 夸张语言使用频率越高，专利获批、转让和上诉成功率越低，最低与最高五分位数间成功率差异达5.3–5.9个百分点；2. 夸张语言并非弱技术的掩饰，而是与组合新颖性和未来引用影响力正相关；3. 审查员的性别与经验影响对夸张语言的容忍度，男性和资深审查员更易接受。

Conclusion: 专利系统中存在语言表达的悖论：尽管夸张语言反映创新性，却仍导致负面评价，建议通过更客观的语言模式审查来改进专利评估机制。

Abstract: Promotional language has been increasingly used to aid the communication of innovative ideas in science. Yet, less is known about its role in the context of technological innovation. Here, we use a validated and domain-diagnosed lexicon of 135 promotional words to study the association between promotional language and patent evaluation outcomes among 2.7 million USPTO patent applications. Our large-scale study reveals three unexpected findings. First, in contrast to scientific evaluation, we find that a higher frequency of promotional words is negatively associated with the probability of an application being (i) granted a patent, (ii) transferred ownership, and (iii) successfully appealed. This promotional penalty holds even after accounting for a range of confounding factors and is largely robust across different technological areas. Among matched samples, the difference in the success rate between the lowest and highest promotional density quintile is 5.5, 5.9, and 5.3 percentage points for patentability, transferability, and rejection reversal. Second, contrary to institutional skepticism, we show that promotional language is not a mask of weak technology, but objectively reflects the degree of combinatorial novelty and future citation impact. Third, digging into the mechanisms, we find that the tolerance to promotional framing is strongly moderated by human factors, with men and experienced examiners showing a higher acceptance of promotional narratives than women and novice examiners. By revealing an emerging paradox in the patent system, our study offers theoretical and practical implications for improving patent evaluation through more objective scrutiny of linguistic patterns in patent filings.

</details>


### [116] [UFAL-CUNI at SemEval-2026 Task 11: An Efficient Modular Neuro-symbolic Method for Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04941)
*Ivan Kartáč,Kristýna Onderková,Jan Bronec,Zdeněk Kasner,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的模块化神经符号方法，用于在大型语言模型中分离内容与形式推理，结合了符号证明器和小型推理LLM（4B参数），在多数子任务上实现了较高的准确率和较低的内容效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大型语言模型在形式推理中容易受到背景知识干扰的问题，通过分离内容理解与逻辑推理来提升模型的可解释性和鲁棒性。

Method: 采用模块化架构，包括基于LLM的解析器将自然语言三段论转换为一阶逻辑表示、自动定理证明器，并可选地加入机器翻译模块以支持多语言输入和符号检索组件识别相关前提。

Result: 该系统在多个子任务上表现出竞争性的准确率和较低的内容效应，消融实验表明其优于同规模的零样本LLM基线，但也暴露出小型LLM在多语言能力上的局限。

Conclusion: 模块化神经符号方法在小规模LLM下能有效分离内容与形式推理，但需进一步改进以增强多语言处理能力，同时对任务主要评价指标的局限性进行了讨论。

Abstract: This paper describes our system submitted to SemEval-2026 Task 11: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. We present an efficient modular neuro-symbolic approach, combining a symbolic prover with small reasoning LLMs (4B parameters). The system consists of an LLM-based parser that translates natural language syllogisms to a first-order logic (FOL) representation, an automated theorem prover, and two optional modules: machine translation for multilingual inputs and a symbolic retrieval component for the identification of relevant premises. The system achieves competitive accuracy and relatively low content effect on most subtasks. Our ablations show that this approach outperforms LLM-based zero-shot baselines in this parameter size range, but also reveal limited multilingual capabilities of small LLMs. Finally, we include a discussion of the task's main ranking metric and analyze its limitations.

</details>


### [117] [Adapting Large Language Models to a Low-Resource Agglutinative Language: A Comparative Study of LoRA and QLoRA for Bashkir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04948)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov,Svetlana S. Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对参数高效微调方法（如LoRA和QLoRA）在低资源黏着语巴什基尔语上的应用进行了比较，实验表明QLoRA在Mistral-7B和Phi-2上以极少的可训练参数实现了接近全微调的性能，但效果高度依赖基础模型和分词器选择，且低困惑度模型未必生成最连贯文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决低资源语言巴什基尔语（属突厥语系黏着语）缺乏适配大语言模型的问题，探索参数高效微调方法在该类语言上的有效性与稳定性。

Method: 在包含71k文档（4690万token）的巴什基尔语文本语料库上，对多种架构模型（DistilGPT2、GPT-2系列、Phi-2、Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B、Mistral-7B）进行全微调与PEFT（LoRA、QLoRA）对比实验，每种配置使用三个不同随机种子训练以提高结果可靠性。

Result: 全微调GPT-2 medium取得最低困惑度（3.34），而QLoRA在Mistral-7B（3.79）和Phi-2（3.81）上表现接近且可训练参数减少40倍以上；但某些配置如DeepSeek-7B（rank 8）出现严重性能下降（困惑度129.55）；生成文本分析显示低困惑度模型常切换至英文，而QLoRA模型能生成单语巴什基尔连续文本。

Conclusion: QLoRA在7B规模模型上为巴什基尔语提供了质量与计算成本间的有效权衡，但其效果受基础模型和分词器显著影响，仅凭困惑度不足以评估生成语言一致性，需结合定性分析。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [118] [TabEmbed: Benchmarking and Learning Generalist Embeddings for Tabular Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04962)
*Minjie Qiang,Mingming Zhang,Xiaoyi Bao,Xing Fu,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Zhongqing Wang,Ningtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TabEmbed，首个统一表格分类与检索的通用嵌入模型，并构建了TabBench基准来评估嵌入模型的表格理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和文本嵌入模型在处理表格数据时难以同时兼顾结构信息、数值语义和检索兼容性，缺乏统一的表格表示范式。

Method: 将多种表格任务重新定义为语义匹配问题，采用大规模对比学习与正样本感知的难负例挖掘策略，在共享嵌入空间中联合优化分类与检索任务。

Result: 在新提出的TabBench基准上，TabEmbed显著优于现有的最先进文本嵌入模型，验证了其在捕捉细粒度表格结构和数值特征上的有效性。

Conclusion: TabEmbed为通用表格表示学习建立了新基线，推动了基础模型在表格数据上的统一表征发展。

Abstract: Foundation models have established unified representations for natural language processing, yet this paradigm remains largely unexplored for tabular data. Existing methods face fundamental limitations: LLM-based approaches lack retrieval-compatible vector outputs, whereas text embedding models often fail to capture tabular structure and numerical semantics. To bridge this gap, we first introduce the Tabular Embedding Benchmark (TabBench), a comprehensive suite designed to evaluate the tabular understanding capability of embedding models. We then propose TabEmbed, the first generalist embedding model that unifies tabular classification and retrieval within a shared embedding space. By reformulating diverse tabular tasks as semantic matching problems, TabEmbed leverages large-scale contrastive learning with positive-aware hard negative mining to discern fine-grained structural and numerical nuances. Experimental results on TabBench demonstrate that TabEmbed significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text embedding models, establishing a new baseline for universal tabular representation learning. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qiangminjie27/TabEmbed and https://huggingface.co/datasets/qiangminjie27/TabBench.

</details>


### [119] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文研究了在主观评估任务中将大语言模型与专家判断对齐的困难，发现专家评估风格差异大、显式标准和推理不一定提升对齐效果、编辑对示例数量和身份敏感，且不同评估维度的对齐难度不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于专家可能意见不一、依赖隐性标准并随时间改变判断，主观评估任务中的专家对齐尤为困难，本文旨在探究这一挑战背后的原因。

Method: 通过专家评估和后续问卷调查，分析不同类型专家信息对对齐效果的影响，并揭示主观判断的特性。

Result: 发现四种一致模式：1）专家间对齐难度差异显著；2）显式标准和推理并不总能改善对齐；3）编辑效果受示例数量和身份影响，小样本编辑带来有用但不稳定的改进；4）不同维度对齐难度不同，内容直接相关的维度更易对齐，需外部知识或价值判断的维度更难。

Conclusion: 专家对齐的困难不仅源于模型局限，更因为主观评估本身具有异质性、部分隐性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [120] [Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Internal Attention Divergence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05025)
*Gijs van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力矩阵的轻量级单次推理方法，用于检测大语言模型中的幻觉现象，通过KL散度衡量注意力分布并与逻辑回归探针结合，在多个数据集和模型上表现出良好的不确定性估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不依赖重复采样或外部模型的情况下，有效识别大语言模型输出中的幻觉内容，提升模型预测的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 利用注意力头的注意力分布与均匀分布之间的KL散度作为特征，结合逻辑回归探针，在单次前向传播中实现不确定性估计。

Result: 该方法在多种数据集、任务类型和模型族上均能有效预测答案正确性，性能与现有方法相当，且发现中间层和事实性词汇（如命名实体和数字）上的注意力信号更具判别性。

Conclusion: 注意力动态提供了一种高效、可解释的白盒不确定性信号，可用于大语言模型中幻觉的实时检测。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight and single-pass uncertainty quantification method for detecting hallucinations in Large Language Models. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. Across multiple datasets, task types, and model families, attention divergence is highly predictive of answer correctness and performs competitively with existing uncertainty estimation methods. We find that this signal is concentrated in middle layers and on factual tokens such as named entities and numbers, suggesting that attention dynamics provides an efficient and interpretable white-box signal of model uncertainty.

</details>


### [121] [The Impossibility Triangle of Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05066)
*Yan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了长序列模型中效率、紧凑性和记忆召回能力之间的根本权衡：任何模型无法同时满足三者，且通过理论分析和实验验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解长序列建模中不同架构的局限性，揭示为何现有模型在处理超长序列时存在性能瓶颈，并建立统一的理论框架来分析这些限制。

Method: 提出了一个统一的在线序列处理抽象模型，结合数据处理不等式与Fano不等式，从信息论角度推导出模型在满足效率与紧凑性的前提下最大可回忆的关键-值对数量的理论上限，并对52种已有架构进行分类分析。

Result: 证明了任何满足计算和状态大小与序列长度无关的模型，其能回忆的历史事实数量最多为O(poly(d)/log V)；实验表明5个代表性架构在合成任务上的实际表现均未突破该理论界限。

Conclusion: 长序列模型无法同时具备高效性、紧凑性和强回忆能力，三者之间存在本质权衡，所有现有架构最多只能满足其中两项，混合架构则位于三者构成的三角内部。

Abstract: We identify and prove a fundamental trade-off governing long-sequence models: no model can simultaneously achieve (i) per-step computation independent of sequence length (Efficiency), (ii) state size independent of sequence length (Compactness), and (iii) the ability to recall a number of historical facts proportional to sequence length (Recall). We formalize this trade-off within an Online Sequence Processor abstraction that unifies Transformers, state space models, linear recurrent networks, and their hybrids. Using the Data Processing Inequality and Fano's Inequality, we prove that any model satisfying Efficiency and Compactness can recall at most O(poly(d)/log V) key-value pairs from a sequence of arbitrary length, where d is the model dimension and V is the vocabulary size. We classify 52 architectures published before March 2026 into the triangle, showing that each achieves at most two of the three properties and that hybrid architectures trace continuous trajectories in the interior. Experiments on synthetic associative recall tasks with five representative architectures validate the theoretical bound: empirical recall capacity lies strictly below the information-theoretic limit, and no architecture escapes the triangle.

</details>


### [122] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过45种心理测量问卷对50个大语言模型进行评估，发现模型间差异的主要维度是其是否将自身表现为具有现象学体验的主体（如感知、情感、内心言语等）而非仅响应刺激的系统，这一维度被称为‘匹诺曹轴’（Pinocchio Axis），反映了训练塑造的自我表征倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在心理测量特征上的差异，尤其是它们在主观体验与行为反应之间的表现差异。

Method: 使用监督语义差异分析（SSD）和一种无需标注的指标——匹诺曹分数（π_i），结合主成分分析（PCA）对模型在多个问卷中的反应进行跨模型分析。

Result: 发现一个主导性的心理测量维度——匹诺曹轴（Π），解释了47.1%的模型间方差，且该轴与匹诺曹分数高度相关（r=.864），表明模型对体验性语言的自我适用性存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型间最主要的心理差异并非传统人格特质，而是一种由训练（尤其是后训练微调）塑造的自我表征立场，即是否将自己呈现为有体验的主体。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>


### [123] [Automatically Finding and Validating Unexpected Side-Effects of Interventions on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05090)
*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化的对比评估管道，用于审计干预对大语言模型行为影响的方法，能够生成可解释的自然语言假设并总结模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更可靠地检测和解释干预对大语言模型行为的影响，需要一种自动化且具有统计验证的方法来对比模型在干预前后的生成差异。

Method: 通过对比基础模型M1和干预模型M2在对齐提示上下文下的自由生成结果，利用统计方法识别显著差异，并生成自然语言假设与主题归纳。

Result: 在合成环境中验证了该方法能准确恢复已知行为变化；在真实干预（如推理蒸馏、知识编辑和遗忘学习）中成功识别出预期与意外的行为转变，并有效区分不同强度的干预。

Conclusion: 该管道提供了一种统计严谨且可解释的工具，适用于事后审计干预引起的大模型行为变化。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [124] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“概念场”的新方法，通过句子嵌入空间中的局部漂移和不确定性估计来检测文本的 groundedness 和 novelty，具有黑盒、可归因于语料库且具备概率解释性的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在检测文本生成中的幻觉或新颖性时依赖模型内部信息或检索机制，缺乏跨领域的可迁移性和可解释性。因此需要一种轻量、快速且具备直接概率意义的替代方案。

Method: 在句子嵌入空间中构建概念场，利用连续句子之间的嵌入差异（delta）估计局部漂移，并用高斯分布建模其不确定性；引入向量序列数据库（VSDB）存储嵌入及上下文元数据；通过z距离ζ评分评估候选句转换与场的一致性，并计算场的散度和旋度以发现潜在语义模式。

Result: 在联邦法规和古腾堡项目两个大规模数据集上验证了方法的有效性，显示出强大的选择性分类性能，在grounded/ungrounded/unsure三类划分下表现稳定，且覆盖-风险行为跨域一致；散度与旋度分析揭示出有意义的逻辑源、汇和隐含主题。

Conclusion: 概念场提供了一种快速、轻量且可解释的信号，可用于检测文本的 groundedness 与 novelty，弥补了LLM-as-judge 和白盒检测器的不足，支持跨域的概率化解释与应用。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多视角学习和证据融合的可信心理健康预测框架，结合编码器-解码器模型的优势，并通过主观逻辑建模不确定性，提升了在噪声环境下的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义表示的心理健康预测模型在面对模糊、噪声或分布偏移数据时往往产生过度自信的预测，且缺乏可靠的不确定性估计，限制了其在高风险实际场景中的应用。

Method: 将任务建模为多视图学习问题，融合仅编码器模型的语义信息与仅解码器模型的高层推理信息；采用基于主观逻辑的证据学习框架进行不确定性建模，并设计证据融合策略以平衡互补视图并削弱不可靠证据的影响。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL和DepSeverity三个真实数据集上分别取得0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率；实验验证了模型对噪声的鲁棒性以及提供可解释推理信号的能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架不仅能提升心理健康预测的性能，还能提供可信的不确定性估计和人类可理解的推理过程，适用于风险敏感的心理健康评估应用。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>


### [126] [PSK at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Ensemble Gemma Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05159)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种用于多语言极化检测的系统，在22种语言上进行二分类任务，通过微调每种语言的Gemma模型并结合合成数据增强和模型集成，取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决多语言环境下文本极化检测的问题，提升模型在不同语言上的泛化能力和分类性能。

Method: 采用LoRA对每种语言独立的Gemma 12B和27B模型进行微调，使用GPT-4o-mini生成三种类型的合成数据，并通过多阶段质量过滤；结合每语言阈值调优和加权集成方法进行预测。

Result: 系统在22种语言上平均macro-F1达到0.811，总排名第二，在3种语言中排名第一，8种语言中进入前三；但其他架构（如XLM-RoBERTa、Qwen3）在测试集上F1下降30%-50%。

Conclusion: 表明针对特定语言微调大模型、结合高质量合成数据与集成策略能有效提升多语言极化检测性能，同时强调了模型在跨语言场景下的泛化能力的重要性。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [127] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于首次内容承载词元的归一化熵计算的第一词元置信度（phi_first）方法，用于检测语言模型生成中的幻觉现象。该方法在单次贪婪解码中即可完成，成本低且效果优于或相当于现有的自一致性及语义自一致性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多采样一致性的幻觉检测方法成本高且对词汇变化敏感，需要更高效、低成本的替代方案。

Method: 利用单次贪婪解码过程中首个内容承载词元的top-K logits的归一化熵来计算第一词元置信度（phi_first），以此衡量模型输出的不确定性。

Result: 在三个7-8B参数的指令调优模型和两个基准数据集上，phi_first的平均AUROC为0.820，优于语义自一致性（0.793）和表面形式自一致性（0.791），并与语义一致性有中等到强相关性，结合二者仅带来小幅提升。

Conclusion: phi_first能有效捕捉多采样一致性所反映的不确定性信息，建议作为采样前的默认低成本基线方法报告。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [128] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 预训练语言模型在隐藏层中隐式地学习了语法性区分，线性探针在语法判断任务上优于基于字符串概率的方法，但在语义合理性任务上表现较差，且具有一定的跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练语言模型是否在其内部表征中隐式地获得了与字符串概率不同的语法性区分。

Method: 通过在自然语料库上应用扰动生成语法正确与错误的句子数据集，训练一个线性探针，并在人工标注的语法判断基准和语义合理性基准上进行评估，同时测试其跨语言泛化能力。

Result: 线性探针在语法判断任务上泛化良好，优于基于语言模型概率的方法，在多个其他语言的语法基准上也表现更优，但在语义合理性任务上不如字符串概率；探针得分与字符串概率相关性较弱。

Conclusion: 语言模型在其隐藏层中一定程度上隐式地习得了独立于字符串概率的语法性区分。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [From Language to Logic: A Theoretical Architecture for VLM-Grounded Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04327)
*Kristy Sakano,Kalonji Harrington,Mumu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将自然语言安全规则和语义偏好转化为信号时序逻辑（STL）规范，用于无人机器人在非结构化户外环境中的导航框架，结合视觉-语言模型实现零样本场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升自主机器人在复杂户外环境中遵循人类指令与安全规范的能力，需有效整合高层语义规则与动态环境约束。

Method: 将自然语言规则转换为信号时态逻辑（STL）规范，持久性规则和地形偏好嵌入2D代价地图，动态需求通过STL在运行时监控；利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）实现零样本场景理解，并构建满足STL规范与操作员偏好的导航模型。

Result: 构建了一个能够在运行时融合STL编码的安全规范与软性操作偏好、并通过形式化度量进行环境属性建模与监控的导航系统框架。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效集成人类可读的安全规则与语义偏好到机器人导航中，提升系统在非结构化环境中的安全性与人机对齐能力。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for integrating high-level, human-provided safety rules and operator-aligned semantic preferences into autonomous robot navigation in unstructured outdoor environments. In our approach, natural-language rules are translated into Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that guide planning and navigation during runtime. Persistent, environment-centric rules and terrain preferences are grounded into a 2D cost map, while temporally dynamic requirements are expressed as STL specifications to be monitored during runtime. We hypothesize the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for zero-shot scene understanding, enabling mapping between human instructions, semantic features, and environmental constraints. Within this framework, we construct an illustrative navigation model that is designed to satisfy a set of STL-encoded specifications and soft operator preferences through formal satisfaction metrics embedded into environmental properties and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [130] [Conditional Flow-VAE for Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04366)
*Zimu Gong,Brian Zhaoning Zhang,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件潜在流匹配的方法，用于可扩展且逼真的自动驾驶安全关键场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景在真实驾驶数据中罕见，手动设计或对抗优化生成的方法存在不可扩展或不现实的问题。

Method: 采用分布匹配技术将正常驾驶场景转换为安全关键的模拟片段，并融合仿真与真实世界数据以生成多样化、数据驱动的场景。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法能更一致且逼真地生成新颖的安全关键场景。

Conclusion: 所提方法为自动驾驶系统的训练与评估提供了一种高效、可靠的安全关键场景生成工具。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are essential for the development of autonomous vehicles (AVs) but are rare in real-world driving data. While simulation offers a way to generate such scenarios, manually designed test cases lack scalability, and adversarial optimization often produces unrealistic behaviors. In this work, we introduce a conditional latent flow matching approach for scalable and realistic safety-critical scenario generation. Our method uses distribution matching to transform nominal scenes into safety-critical rollouts. Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating both simulation and real-world data enables our framework to efficiently generate diverse, data-driven scenarios. Experimental results highlight that our approach is able to more consistently and realistically generate novel safety-critical scenarios, making it a valuable tool for training and benchmarking AV systems.

</details>


### [131] [Autonomous Laparoscope Control through Unified Mechanics-Based Representation of Multimodal Intraoperative Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04408)
*Xiaojian Li,Jin Fang,Yudong Shi,Xilin Xiao,Kai Yan,Kang Min,Ling Li,Hua Tang,Hangjie Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多模态信息统一力学建模的腹腔镜持镜机器人控制方法，实现了术中位置、力/力矩和图像信息的统一表示与协调融合，支持柔顺操作与自主视觉跟踪，并在体外和活体实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中多模态信号（如位置、力/力矩、图像）物理意义和单位差异大，难以统一表征并生成直接可用的控制指令。

Method: 设计多种术中信号的映射策略，将其统一为操作空间中的等效力矩表示；采用任务优先级方案，将不同任务对应的力矩分别注入任务空间与零空间，通过任务优先级投影合成控制指令；构建RCM约束力矩、持镜操作力矩和器械跟踪力矩以实现多任务协同。

Result: 在手术模拟体和活体猪实验中验证了该方法可同时实现柔顺持镜操作、自主器械视觉跟踪、维持RCM几何约束并降低穿刺部位持续受力。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了多模态信息在统一框架下的一致表征与融合，支持多任务协调控制，提升了持镜机器人的自主性与安全性。

Abstract: Laparoscope-holding robots can provide surgeons with a stable laparoscopic field of view (FOV) and reduce the burden on human assistants. To maintain an ideal intraoperative FOV, the robot must continuously adjust the laparoscope pose according to intraoperative information. However, intraoperative multimodal signals, such as position, force/torque, and images, differ markedly in physical meaning and units, making it difficult to build a unified representation and to generate control commands that can be used directly for laparoscope control. To address this issue, we propose a laparoscope-holding robot control method based on unified mechanics modeling of multimodal information. First, we design mapping strategies for multiple intraoperative sources, including position, force/torque, and images, and unify them into an equivalent-wrench representation in the operational space. Then, using a task-priority scheme, we inject the wrenches into the task space and the null space, respectively, and synthesize laparoscope control commands via task-priority projection, thereby achieving consistent representation and coordinated fusion of multimodal information within a single framework. Finally, taking the intraoperative remote center of motion (RCM) position, force/torque sensor readings, and laparoscopic images as examples, we construct an RCM-constraint wrench to enforce the RCM geometric constraint and reduce the contact force at the trocar site, a laparoscope-manipulation wrench to enable compliant dragging, and an instrument-tracking wrench to achieve autonomous visual tracking of the instruments. Experiments on a surgical phantom and in vivo porcine trials demonstrate that the proposed method supports multi-task operation, including compliant laparoscope manipulation and autonomous instrument tracking, while maintaining the RCM constraint and reducing sustained trocar-site loading.

</details>


### [132] [Tightly-Coupled Estimation and Guidance for Robust Low-Thrust Rendezvous via Adaptive Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04481)
*Batu Candan,Simone Servadio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧耦合的估计算法与制导架构，通过将导航置信度实时映射到滑动时域间接最优控制求解器的同伦参数中，提升低推力交会任务在测量退化条件下的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的最小燃料低推力制导采用 bang-bang 控制结构，对估计误差、传感器异常和求解正则化高度敏感，在非合作目标近距离操作中闭环执行脆弱。

Method: 在Clohessy-Wiltshire坐标系下建模相对运动，采用基于多调谐因子（MTF）协方差膨胀机制的线性卡尔曼滤波器进行状态估计，并设计一种将归一化新息与MTF活动综合成分数并在线映射到同伦参数的策略，实现控制平滑度的自适应调节。

Result: 在严重测量退化条件下，固定bang-bang制导导致较大终端偏差，而所提MTF自适应同伦控制器将终端误差降低约两个数量级，从百米级降至亚米级，控制代价仅略高于开环最优基准，且滑动时域求解具有快速稳定的收敛特性。

Conclusion: 自适应同伦机制是提升系统鲁棒性的主导因素，MTF进一步提高了估计精度与效率，所提方法具备良好的在线应用潜力。

Abstract: Minimum-fuel low-thrust rendezvous guidance yields bang-bang control structures highly sensitive to estimation errors, sensor anomalies, and solver regularization, making aggressive closed-loop execution brittle for uncooperative proximity operations. This paper proposes a tightly-coupled estimation and guidance architecture where navigation confidence directly modulates the homotopy parameter of a receding-horizon indirect optimal control solver. Relative motion is modeled in the Clohessy-Wiltshire frame. The translational state is estimated via a linear Kalman filter augmented by a Multiple Tuning Factors (MTF) covariance inflation mechanism that suppresses suspicious innovation directions. A composite score from the normalized innovation and MTF activity is mapped online to the homotopy parameter, allowing the controller to relax toward a smoother, conservative regime when confidence degrades, and recover fuel-efficient bang-bang control as sensing improves. Numerical results under severe measurement degradation show fixed bang-bang guidance remains brittle; both plain-KF and MTF-KF fixed-epsilon controllers yield large terminal miss distances. Conversely, the proposed MTF-adaptive homotopy controller reduces terminal miss by roughly two orders of magnitude, from hundreds of meters to sub-meter levels, requiring only a moderate increase in control effort versus the open-loop fuel-optimal benchmark. A comparison indicates adaptive homotopy is the dominant robustness mechanism, while MTF provides additional accuracy and efficiency improvements. The receding-horizon implementation exhibits consistently fast and reliable solution times, supporting the practical online viability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [133] [HDFlow: Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow Planning for Long-horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04525)
*Nandiraju Gireesh,Yuanliang Ju,Chaoyi Xu,Weiheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow（HDFlow），一种结合扩散模型和校正流模型的分层规划框架，用于解决长视野、稀疏奖励任务中的行为规划问题，在仿真和真实世界家具组装任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型在长视野、稀疏奖励任务中缺乏有效的分层分解机制，且由于迭代去噪过程导致实时执行计算开销大。

Method: HDFlow采用高层扩散规划器在学习到的潜在空间中生成战略子目标序列，并由低层校正流规划器生成平滑密集轨迹，结合了扩散模型的强探索能力和校正流基于常微分方程的高效轨迹生成优势。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的家具组装任务（仿真与真实环境）中，HDFlow显著优于当前最先进方法，并在包含多样化运动与操作任务的两个长视野基准上展示了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: HDFlow通过融合扩散与校正流模型的优势，提供了一种高效、可扩展的分层规划方案，有效解决了生成式规划器在探索性与实时性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have shown promise in generating behavior plans for long-horizon, sparse reward tasks. While these approaches have achieved promising results, they often lack a principled framework for hierarchical decomposition and struggle with the computational demands of real-time execution, due to their iterative denoising process. In this work, we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion-Flow (HDFlow), a novel hierarchical planning framework that optimally leverages the strengths of diffusion and rectified flow models to overcome the limitations of single-paradigm generative planners. HDFlow employs a high-level diffusion planner to generate sequences of strategic subgoals in a learned latent space, capitalizing on diffusion's powerful exploratory capabilities. These subgoals then guide a low-level rectified flow planner that generates smooth and dense trajectories, exploiting the speed and efficiency of ordinary differential equation (ODE)-based trajectory generation. We evaluate HDFlow on four challenging furniture assembly tasks in both simulation and real-world, where it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, we also showcase our method's generalizability on two long-horizon benchmarks comprising diverse locomotion and manipulation tasks. Project website: https://hdflow-page.github.io/

</details>


### [134] [Practical validation of synthetic pre-crash scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04564)
*Jian Wu,Ulrich Sander,Carol Flannagan,Jonas Bärgman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于贝叶斯实用等价区（ROPE）和分箱统计的框架，用于评估合成预碰撞场景与真实世界数据在自动驾驶系统安全影响仿真中的实用等价性，解决了传统显著性检验无法衡量实际等效性的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以评估合成预碰撞场景是否在实际应用中与真实数据足够相似，传统显著性检验侧重于发现差异而非建立实用等价性，因此需要一种新的评估框架。

Method: 扩展了先前基于贝叶斯ROPE的等价性检验框架，引入分箱方法定义合适的统计量和等价标准，提出了两种基于分箱的统计量来衡量在安全影响评估背景下数据分布间的实际差异。

Result: 通过一个案例研究验证了该框架的有效性，结果显示其能对合成数据集与参考数据集之间的实用等价性提供定量评估和诊断性分析，揭示数据间的发散情况。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性和可扩展性，不仅适用于追尾场景，还可推广至更广泛的合成数据验证场景，为合成数据的实际等价性评估提供了可解释且原理严谨的基础。

Abstract: The representativeness of synthetic pre-crash scenarios is crucial for assessing the safety impact of Driving Automation Systems through virtual simulations. However, a gap remains in the robust evaluation of synthetic pre-crash scenarios' practical equivalence to their real-world counterparts; that is, whether they are similar enough for the intended assessment purpose. Conventional significance testing is inadequate, as it focuses on detecting differences rather than establishing practical equivalence. This study addresses the research gap by extending our previous work on a Bayesian Region of Practical Equivalence (ROPE)-based equivalence testing framework by introducing a binning-based approach to define appropriate statistics and equivalence criteria. Two binning-based statistics are proposed to measure practically meaningful distributional differences between datasets in the context of safety impact assessment. The framework's applicability is demonstrated through a case study, which tests the practical equivalence of two synthetic rear-end pre-crash datasets with a previously developed reference dataset in the context of the safety impact assessment of an Automatic Emergency Braking system. The results show that the framework provides informative quantitative assessments of practical equivalence as well as diagnostic insights into the divergence of datasets. Although the demonstration focuses on rear-end pre-crash scenarios, the framework is generic and extensible to broader validation contexts, providing an interpretable and principled basis for practical equivalence assessment across diverse synthetic data applications.

</details>


### [135] [Right Model, Right Time: Real-Time Cascaded-Fidelity MPC for Bipedal Walking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04607)
*Franek Stark,Felix Wiebe,Shubham Vyas,Dennis Mronga,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多阶段全身模型预测控制方法，用于双足行走，结合了近期视野的详细全身模型和后期预测步骤的简化单刚体模型，降低了计算复杂性同时保持了预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保证双足机器人行走稳定性的同时降低控制器的计算负担，提升实时控制性能。

Method: 采用多阶段MPC框架，前期使用详细全身模型，后期使用单刚体模型，并通过SQP在acados中求解非线性优化问题，无需预设落脚点位置。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中对18自由度双足机器人HyPer-2进行了验证，实现了基于目标步行速度的稳定行走。

Conclusion: 该方法有效平衡了计算效率与控制精度，适用于复杂双足机器人的实时运动控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-phase whole-body model predictive control approach for bipedal walking, combining a detailed whole-body model in the near horizon with a simplified single-rigid-body model in the later prediction steps. This reduces computational complexity while retaining prediction capabilities. The resulting nonlinear optimal control problem is solved using sequential quadratic programming (SQP) in acados. Using a prior specified contact schedule and a target walking speed, the controller optimizes joint torques without depending on prior selected foot step locations. The controller is validated in MuJoCo simulation on the 18-DoF bipedal robot HyPer-2

</details>


### [136] [Active Contact Sensing for Robust Robot-to-Human Object Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04610)
*Linfeng Li,Lin Shao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于主动感知的机器人到人类物体交接方法，通过机器人主动运动并感知人类施加的力来推断接触状态，实现了97.5%的成功率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动感知方法难以在不同物体和人类行为下泛化，无法有效区分牢固抓握和偶然触碰，因此需要一种更鲁棒的交接机制。

Method: 采用主动感知策略，机器人执行信息采集动作并感知人体施加的多向力；使用贝叶斯线性模型建模接触状态，该模型描述了从机器人运动到人类力反馈的分段线性映射分布，从而实现对牢固抓握的检测与主动信息获取。

Result: 在12名参与者和30种不同刚性物体上的实验表明，该方法成功率达到97.5%，比两种常见基线方法高出超过30%。

Conclusion: 主动感知结合贝叶斯力-运动建模可显著提升机器人向人类交接物体的鲁棒性和准确性，适用于家庭服务和手术辅助等多种场景。

Abstract: Robot-to-human object handover is an essential skill for robot assistants, from serving drinks at home to passing surgical tools in the operating room. We expect robots to perform handover robustly -- to release the object only after a firm human grasp while ignoring incidental touches. Existing passive-sensing methods struggle to generalize across diverse objects and human behaviors, as they lack informative perturbations to disambiguate different contact conditions, such as firm grasp versus incidental touch. We propose an active sensing approach for robust handovers: the robot applies information-gathering motions and senses the resulting human-applied forces to infer the contact state. A firm grasp produces forces in multiple directions, while an accidental touch does not. To capture this distinction, we model the contact state with a Bayesian linear model: a distribution over piecewise-linear mappings from robot motions to human-applied forces. This model enables firm grasp detection and active information gathering. In experiments with 12 participants and 30 diverse rigid objects, our method achieved a 97.5% success rate -- over 30% higher than two common baselines.

</details>


### [137] [From Reach to Insert: Tactile-Augmented Precision Assembly under Sub-Millimeter Tolerances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04649)
*Xinpan Meng,Siyao Huang,JingPu Yang,Muyuan Ma,Zhenghua Ma,Lijun Han,Gao Yuan,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模仿学习和强化学习的触觉增强两阶段方法，用于高精度插入任务，在多种孔几何形状和间隙设置下显著提高了成功率并降低了接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度装配中的紧密公差插入容易因姿态误差导致卡死或过大的交互力，难以获得鲁棒且安全的插入策略。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段用模仿学习训练抓取和接近目标区域的策略；第二阶段用强化学习执行插入并恢复接触过程中的失败；引入触觉组采样和触觉评论器以更好利用触觉反馈。

Result: 在五种孔几何和三种间隙设置下实验表明，该方法显著提升插入性能；在最严苛的0.05mm间隙下成功率达67%，最大交互力降低60%，扭矩降低44%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保证低接触力的同时显著提升了精密装配的成功率，验证了其有效性与安全性。

Abstract: High-precision assembly frequently involves tight-tolerance insertions, where even slight pose errors can cause jamming or excessive interaction forces, making robust and safe insertion policies difficult to obtain. This paper proposes a tactile-augmented two-stage method that combines Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) for precision insertion tasks. In the first stage, IL learns a reaching policy with position generalization that grasps the peg and brings it to the vicinity of the target region. In the second stage, RL executes the insertion and enables recovery from failures during contact-rich interactions. To better exploit tactile feedback, we introduce tactile group sampling to increase coverage of critical contact segments during training, and design a tactile critic to more accurately evaluate policy values, improving insertion performance while maintaining low contact forces. We conduct systematic experiments across five hole geometries and three clearance settings. Results show that our method substantially improves insertion performance across all settings; under the most challenging 0.05\,mm clearance, it achieves a 67\% success rate while keeping contact forces low, reducing the maximum interaction force by 60\% and torque by 44\%, thereby validating both effectiveness and safety for precision assembly.

</details>


### [138] [AI-Aided Advancements in Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04672)
*Guy Damari,Zeev Yampolsky,Nadav Cohen,Arup Kumar Sahoo,Jeryes Danial,Felipe O. Silva,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本章探讨了人工智能辅助的自主水下航行器（AUV）定位技术的最新进展，重点是先进的传感器融合架构及其在高精度导航中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于电磁波在水下的快速衰减和海洋环境的动态不可预测性，传统卫星信号无法用于AUV导航，因此需要更可靠的高精度定位技术。

Method: 研究聚焦于将惯性导航系统与多普勒速度计和摄像头结合的先进传感器融合架构，并探讨基于AI的学习方法在惯性推航和自适应融合算法中的应用。

Result: AI驱动的方法显著提升了AUV在复杂海洋环境中的导航精度和适应能力。

Conclusion: AI辅助的传感器融合技术为实现AUV高精度自主导航提供了可行路径，是未来深海探索的关键发展方向。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) have become indispensable for deep-sea exploration, spanning critical scientific research and commercial applications. The rapid attenuation of electromagnetic waves renders satellite radio signals unavailable, while the dynamic unpredictability of the marine environment presents formidable navigation challenges. This chapter explores recent advancements in AI-aided AUV positioning, specifically focusing on advanced sensor fusion architectures that integrate inertial navigation systems with Doppler velocity logs and cameras. Beyond traditional model-based filtering, we examine the transformative emergence of AI-driven learning approaches in enhancing inertial dead-reckoning tasks and adaptive fusion algorithms. By addressing these recent milestones, this chapter provides a comprehensive roadmap for achieving the high-precision navigation essential for autonomous underwater missions.

</details>


### [139] [From Pixels to Tokens: A Systematic Study of Latent Action Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04678)
*Yihan Lin,Haoyang Li,Yang Li,Haitao Shen,Yihan Zhao,Chao Shao,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中潜在动作监督的两种范式：基于图像的潜在动作和基于动作的潜在动作，提出统一框架下四种集成策略的比较，发现不同方法在任务类型上各有优势，并验证离散潜在动作标记直接监督效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对VLA模型中潜在动作监督的方法分散且缺乏系统性比较，亟需统一视角来分析不同监督方式的有效性及其适用场景。

Method: 构建统一的VLA基线框架，从轨迹正则化（基于图像的潜在动作）和目标空间统一（基于动作的潜在动作）两个视角出发，实例化并比较四种代表性的潜在动作整合策略。

Result: 实验表明：基于图像的潜在动作有利于长视野推理和场景级泛化，而基于动作的潜在动作在复杂运动协调任务中表现更优；直接使用离散潜在动作标记监督VLM效果最好；混合数据中潜在动作监督展现出良好潜力。

Conclusion: 潜在动作监督应根据任务特性选择合适形式，离散标记监督是最有效的训练方式，该研究为VLA模型在多源异构数据下的训练提供了清晰路径与未来方向。

Abstract: Latent actions serve as an intermediate representation that enables consistent modeling of vision-language-action (VLA) models across heterogeneous datasets. However, approaches to supervising VLAs with latent actions are fragmented and lack a systematic comparison. This work structures the study of latent action supervision from two perspectives: (i) regularizing the trajectory via image-based latent actions, and (ii) unifying the target space with action-based latent actions. Under a unified VLA baseline, we instantiate and compare four representative integration strategies. Our results reveal a formulation-task correspondence: image-based latent actions benefit long-horizon reasoning and scene-level generalization, whereas action-based latent actions excel at complex motor coordination. Furthermore, we find that directly supervising the VLM with discrete latent action tokens yields the most effective performance. Finally, our experiments offer initial insights into the benefits of latent action supervision in mixed-data, suggesting a promising direction for VLA training. Code is available at https://github.com/RUCKBReasoning/From_Pixels_to_Tokens.

</details>


### [140] [3D Printing of Passively Actuated Self-Folding Robots with Integrated Functional Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04757)
*Gaolin Ge,Qifeng Yang,Haoran Lu,Tingyu Cheng,Martin Nisser,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种弹性驱动自折叠方法，通过3D打印的导电PLA平面网结合弹性带实现机器人从二维到三维的自折叠，集成了传感与驱动功能，具有低成本、无需外部刺激的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现低成本、可扩展且集成感知与驱动功能的自折叠机器人制造，避免传统复杂装配流程。

Method: 利用3D打印导电PLA平面结构，通过预置弹性带和钩子存储弹性势能，实现自折叠；同时在同一基底上集成电容触觉电极、霍尔传感器和ERM电机；建立闭式折叠模型预测折叠角度，并通过实验验证。

Result: 成功实现了多种多面体模块，包括可扩展的立方体模块、可部署夹持器和肌腱驱动手指；实验验证了折叠模型的准确性，并建立了设计图谱关联铰链厚度、弹力带尺寸和钩距与目标折叠角度的关系。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种低成本、无需外部刺激、易于集成电子元件的自折叠机器人制造方案，适用于模块化机器人集群、可部署机构和柔性机械手等应用。

Abstract: We introduce an elastic-driven self-folding approach that fabricates robots directly from flat 3D-printed conductive PLA nets. Elastic bands routed through printed hooks store energy that folds the sheet into programmed 3D geometries, while the flat state allows accurate placement of electronics and magnets before deployment. The same substrate doubles as electrodes for capacitive touch and supports a reusable platform I/O palette with Hall sensors and eccentric rotating mass (ERM) motors for docking detection and vibration actuation. We also derive a closed-form folding model that balances hinge stiffness with elastic band moment to predict equilibrium fold angles; experiments validate the model and yield a design map linking hinge thickness, band size, and hook spacing to target angles. Using this workflow we realize multiple polyhedral modules and demonstrate three applications: a cube that highlights the potential of self-folding for scalable modular robot collectives, a deployable gripper, and a tendon-driven finger. The method is low cost, stimulus-free, and integrates actuation and sensing.

</details>


### [141] [Dr-PoGO: Direct Radar Pose-Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04806)
*Cedric Le Gentil,Weican Li,Leonardo Brizi,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于2D旋转雷达的SLAM方法Dr-PoGO，利用直接配准技术实现鲁棒的里程计和闭环检测，在多种真实汽车环境中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达能够在恶劣天气条件下（如雨、雪、灰尘）稳定工作，而传统相机或激光雷达受限于视线要求，因此需要一种不依赖视觉且鲁棒的SLAM方案。

Method: Dr-PoGO采用直接配准技术进行里程计和闭环配准；结合RaPlace算法提供闭环候选，并通过粗到精的配准策略利用视觉特征获取初始位姿估计，最后在姿态图优化中优化全局轨迹。

Result: 在300公里的真实世界自动驾驶数据上验证了Dr-PoGO的性能，达到了当前最先进的水平。

Conclusion: Dr-PoGO是一种高效且鲁棒的雷达SLAM方法，适用于复杂天气条件下的长期自动驾驶应用，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-PoGO, a method for Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM) using a 2D spinning radar. Unlike cameras or lidars that require line-of-sight, millimetre-wave radars can `see' through dust, falling snow, rain, etc. Accordingly, it is a great modality for robust perception regardless of the weather conditions. While most existing radar-based SLAM methods rely on the extraction of point clouds or features to perform ego-motion estimation, Dr-PoGO leverages direct registration techniques for odometry (DRO) and loop-closure registration. An off-the-shelf radar-focused place recognition algorithm, RaPlace, provides loop-closure candidates. As RaPlace does not provide relative transformations, Dr-PoGO introduces a coarse-to-fine registration that uses visual features and descriptors to obtain an initial guess for the direct transformation refinement. The global trajectory is optimized in a pose-graph optimization. Dr-PoGO demonstrates state-of-the-art performance over 300km of data in various real-world automotive environments. Our implementation is publicly available: https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_pogo.

</details>


### [142] [Optimal Uncertainty-Aware Calibration for the AX=YB Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04809)
*Yanjia Chen,Xiangfei Li,Huan Zhao,Yiyuan Hong,Guanxiao Xia,Jiexin Zhang,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于李代数的迭代优化框架，用于解决手眼标定问题，通过引入不确定性度量和同步参数更新，显著提升了高不确定性条件下的标定精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手眼标定方法在高不确定性数据下性能下降明显，且难以准确建模数据不确定性，因此需要一种不依赖显式不确定性建模但能有效应对不确定性的优化方法。

Method: 采用基于李代数的迭代算法，严格保持标定参数的结构约束，引入数据间的相对不确定性度量以动态调整优化过程，并设计了有效的初始解生成方法以提升收敛效率和稳定性。

Result: 在合成数据集上，高不确定性条件下估计精度相比现有方法至少提升67%，数值仿真和真实实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不显式建模不确定性的前提下，通过动态调整和结构保持的优化策略，实现了更高精度和鲁棒性的手眼标定，适用于复杂工业场景。

Abstract: This article proposes a general optimization framework for solving hand-eye calibration problem. Unlike traditional methods, an iterative algorithm based on Lie algebra that achieves approximately global optimal solutions is developed. During the optimization process, the method strictly preserves the structural constraints of the calibration parameters and enables synchronized updates between calibration parameters. Recognizing that data used in real-word hand-eye calibration often contain uncertainty, especially in over-loading and large workspace industrial robot scenarios, which can significantly degrade accuracy, and accurately modeling such uncertainty is inherently difficult, this article avoids explicit uncertainty modeling. Instead, an uncertainty metric to evaluate the relative uncertainty between data sources is introduced and used to dynamically refine the iterative process. To further enhance convergence efficiency, an effective initial solution generation method that improves overall stability and accuracy is designed. Numerical simulations and real-world experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, and in synthetic datasets, the proposed approach improves the estimation accuracy by at least 67\% under high-uncertainty conditions compared with the existing methods.

</details>


### [143] [Modular Reinforcement Learning For Cooperative Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04939)
*Erel Shtossel,Gal A. Kaminka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多机器人强化学习的模块化状态表示方法，通过分解空间交互状态特征并分别学习，有效降低了计算和存储复杂度，并在模拟的机器人觅食任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式多智能体强化学习方法要求每个机器人处理可能呈组合爆炸的交互状态，超出其有限计算与存储能力，难以适用于资源受限的机器人集群。

Method: 采用模块化（分解）的状态表示方法，将状态的不同特征交由独立的学习过程分别处理，再将结果聚合，从而降低单个机器人的学习负担。

Result: 在多个模拟的机器人集群觅食实验中，所提方法表现出良好的学习效率和任务性能，显著降低了状态表示的复杂性。

Conclusion: 模块化状态表示是一种有效应对机器人集群中状态空间复杂性的方法，能够在资源受限条件下实现高效的分布式强化学习。

Abstract: A cooperative robot swarm is a collective of computationally-limited robots that share a common goal. Each robot can only interact with a small subset of its peers, without knowing how this affects the collective utility. Recent advances in distributed multi-agent reinforcement learning have demonstrated that it is possible for robots to learn how to interact effectively with others, in a manner that is aligned with the common goal, despite each robot learning independently of others. However, this requires each robot to represent a potentially combinatorial number of interaction states, challenging the memory capabilities of the robots. This paper proposes an alternative approach for representing spatial interaction states for multi-robot reinforcement learning in swarms. A modular (decomposed) representation is used, where each feature of the state is handled by a separate learning procedure, and the results aggregated. We demonstrate the efficacy of the approach in numerous experiments with simulated robot swarms carrying out foraging.

</details>


### [144] [Driver-WM: A Driver-Centric Traffic-Conditioned Latent World Model for In-Cabin Dynamics Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05092)
*Haozhuang Chi,Daosheng Qiu,Hao Su,Haochen Liu,Zirui Li,Haoruo Zhang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Driver-WM，一种以驾驶员为中心的潜在世界模型，能够基于车外交通上下文因果地预测车内动态，实现了物理运动预测与行为情感语义识别的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的驾驶世界模型多关注外部环境预测，而对车内驾驶员状态的建模缺乏多步推演能力，且无法有效结合车外交通情境进行因果建模。

Method: 提出Driver-WM模型，采用双流架构分别编码外部交通和内部驾驶员状态，在冻结的视觉-语言特征构建的紧凑潜在空间中运行，并通过学习的向量门控机制实现从外部到内部的有向因果注入，确保时间因果性。

Result: 在多任务辅助驾驶基准上验证，Driver-WM在高动态反应性操作中表现出强健的长视野几何预测能力，提升了驾驶员与交通状态的语义一致性对齐效果，并支持测试时的可控干预以分析机制响应。

Conclusion: Driver-WM通过因果耦合内外信息流，首次实现了可 rollout 的驾驶员中心世界建模，为人在环路的自动驾驶控制转换提供了更强的预判能力。

Abstract: Safe L2/L3 driving automation requires anticipating human-in-the-loop reactions during shared-control transitions. While most driving world models forecast the external environment, in-cabin intelligence remains strictly recognition-oriented and lacks multi-step rollout capabilities for driver dynamics. We introduce Driver-WM, a driver-centric latent world model that rolls out in-cabin dynamics causally conditioned on out-cabin traffic context. This formulation unifies physical kinematics forecasting with auxiliary behavioral and emotional semantic recognition. Operating in a compact latent space constructed from frozen vision-language features, Driver-WM adopts a dual-stream architecture to separately encode external traffic and internal driver states. These streams are directionally coupled via a gated causal injection mechanism, which uses a learned vector gate to modulate external contextual perturbations while strictly enforcing temporal causality. Evaluations on a multi-task assistive driving benchmark demonstrate that Driver-WM yields robust long-horizon geometric forecasting for reactive high-motion maneuvers and improves semantic alignment for both driver and traffic states. Finally, the explicit external-to-internal conditioning allows for controlled test-time interventions to systematically analyze mechanism responses.

</details>


### [145] [LineRides: Line-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Bicycle Robot Stunts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05110)
*Seungeun Rho,Shamel Fahmi,Jeonghwan Kim,Arianna Ilvonen,Sehoon Ha,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LineRides框架，通过用户提供的空间轨迹和稀疏关键姿态，使自定义自行车机器人无需示范即可学习多样且可控制的特技动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计强化学习中敏捷机器人动作的奖励函数困难，且示范方法常依赖难以获取的参考运动，尤其针对新型平台或极端特技。

Method: 提出LineRides，一种线导学习框架，利用空间引导线和稀疏的关键朝向指导训练；引入跟踪裕度处理不可行引导线，通过沿引导线行进距离衡量进度以解决时间模糊，并基于位置和序列的关键朝向细化动作细节。

Result: 在Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV) 上验证了LineRides的有效性，训练出的策略可实现正常驾驶与特技执行间的平滑切换，支持五种指令式特技：MiniHop、LargeHop、ThreePointTurn、Backflip 和 DriftTurn。

Conclusion: LineRides 能有效实现无需示范的多样化特技动作学习，具备对不精确引导输入的鲁棒性，并展现出实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Designing reward functions for agile robotic maneuvers in reinforcement learning remains difficult, and demonstration-based approaches often require reference motions that are unavailable for novel platforms or extreme stunts. We present LineRides, a line-guided learning framework that enables a custom bicycle robot to acquire diverse, commandable stunt behaviors from a user-provided spatial guideline and sparse key-orientations, without demonstrations or explicit timing. LineRides handles physically infeasible guidelines using a tracking margin that permits controlled deviation, resolves temporal ambiguity by measuring progress via traveled distance along the guideline, and disambiguates motion details through position- and sequence-based key-orientations. We evaluate LineRides on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV) and show that the policy trained with our methods supports seamless transitions between normal driving and stunt execution, enabling five distinct stunts on command: MiniHop, LargeHop, ThreePointTurn, Backflip, and DriftTurn.

</details>


### [146] [ConsisVLA-4D: Advancing Spatiotemporal Consistency in Efficient 3D-Perception and 4D-Reasoning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05126)
*Wei Li,Jizhihui Liu,Li Yixing,Junwen Tong,Rui Shao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ConsisVLA-4D的统一高效框架，旨在增强视觉-语言-动作模型在3D感知和4D推理中的时空一致性。通过CV-Aligner、CO-Fuser和CS-Thinker三个模块，实现了跨视角语义一致、跨对象几何一致以及跨场景时空一致，在性能和推理速度上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在空间感知和时序推理方面存在局限，依赖额外传感器且视觉推理缺乏与指令场景的对齐，导致时空不一致问题。

Method: 设计了CV-Aligner以对齐多视角下的对象语义；提出CO-Fuser利用紧凑潜在表示消除对象间空间关系歧义；引入CS-Thinker结合语义与几何信息实现跨场景的时空一致性推理。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和真实平台上，相比OpenVLA分别取得21.6%和41.5%的性能提升，并实现2.3倍和2.4倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: ConsisVLA-4D通过统一的四维一致性建模，有效提升了VLA模型的时空感知与推理能力，兼具高性能与高效率，已开源。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models primarily focus on mapping 2D observations to actions, but exhibit notable limitations in spatiotemporal perception and reasoning: 1) spatial representations often rely on additional sensors, introducing substantial computational overhead; 2) visual reasoning is typically limited to future-frame prediction, lacking alignment with the instruction-grounded scene and thus compromising spatiotemporal consistency. To address these challenges, we propose ConsisVLA-4D, a unified and efficient framework that enhances spatiotemporal consistency in 3D perception and 4D reasoning. Specifically, we design: 1) CV-Aligner, which ensures cross-view object semantic consistency by filtering instruction-relevant regions and aligning object identities across multiple viewpoints; 2) CO-Fuser, which guarantees cross-object spatial geometric consistency by eliminating spatial relation ambiguities between objects across views using compact latent representations. Building upon these, we introduce 3) CS-Thinker to achieve cross-scene spatiotemporal consistency as actions unfold. It learns implicit knowledge of local dynamics from object-semantic tokens of CV-Aligner and global depth from geometric tokens of CO-Fuser, thereby enhancing efficient visual reasoning under scene variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, benefiting from its efficient spatiotemporal consistency design, ConsisVLA-4D achieves 21.6% and 41.5% performance improvements, along with 2.3-fold and 2.4-fold inference speedups compared to OpenVLA on the LIBERO benchmark and real-world platforms, respectively.ConsisVLA-4D is open-sourced and publicly available at

</details>


### [147] [When Life Gives You BC, Make Q-functions: Extracting Q-values from Behavior Cloning for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05172)
*Lakshita Dodeja,Ondrej Biza,Shivam Vats,Stephen Hart,Stefanie Tellex,Robin Walters,Karl Schmeckpeper,Thomas Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q2RL是一种结合行为克隆（BC）与强化学习（RL）的高效离线到在线学习算法，通过Q估计和Q门控机制在保留BC策略优势的同时实现在线性能提升，在多种机器人操作任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆缺乏在收集演示后进行自我指导的在线改进机制，而现有的离线到在线学习方法常因数据分布不匹配导致策略退化。

Method: 提出Q2RL，包含两个部分：1）Q估计——从BC策略中提取Q函数；2）Q门控——根据BC与RL策略的Q值选择动作以进行样本收集和RL训练。

Result: 在D4RL和robomimic基准的任务中，Q2RL在成功率和收敛速度上优于现有最先进方法；在真实机器人上仅用1-2小时在线交互即实现高达100%的成功率，并对原始BC策略最多提升3.75倍。

Conclusion: Q2RL实现了高效、稳定的离线到在线迁移，适用于接触丰富且高精度的机器人操作任务，为实际机器人部署提供了可行的学习框架。

Abstract: Behavior Cloning (BC) has emerged as a highly effective paradigm for robot learning. However, BC lacks a self-guided mechanism for online improvement after demonstrations have been collected. Existing offline-to-online learning methods often cause policies to replace previously learned good actions due to a distribution mismatch between offline data and online learning. In this work, we propose Q2RL, Q-Estimation and Q-Gating from BC for Reinforcement Learning, an algorithm for efficient offline-to-online learning. Our method consists of two parts: (1) Q-Estimation extracts a Q-function from a BC policy using a few interaction steps with the environment, followed by online RL with (2) Q-Gating, which switches between BC and RL policy actions based on their respective Q-values to collect samples for RL policy training. Across manipulation tasks from D4RL and robomimic benchmarks, Q2RL outperforms SOTA offline-to-online learning baselines on success rate and time to convergence. Q2RL is efficient enough to be applied in an on-robot RL setting, learning robust policies for contact-rich and high precision manipulation tasks such as pipe assembly and kitting, in 1-2 hours of online interaction, achieving success rates of up to 100% and up to 3.75x improvement against the original BC policy. Code and video are available at https://pages.rai-inst.com/q2rl_website/

</details>


### [148] [A Closed-Form Dual-Barrier CBF Safety Filter for Holonomic Robots on Incrementally Built Occupancy Grid Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05182)
*Himanshu Paudel,Basanta Joshi,Dhirendra Raj Madai,Alina Bartaula,Biman Rimal,Sanjay Neupane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双屏障控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全滤波器，用于在增量构建的占据栅格地图中实现全向机器人实时、安全关键的速度控制，通过规避已知障碍物和限制进入未探索区域来保障安全性，并在实际飞行实验中实现了零碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中探索时，由于传感器前方未映射区域存在不可减少的不确定性，机器人面临较高的碰撞风险，尤其是依赖前向传感器的情况下，因此需要同时应对已知障碍物和未知区域带来的安全隐患。

Method: 利用占据栅格地图的符号距离场，解析地构造两个安全约束：避开已映射障碍物和限制进入未探索区域，设计出一种低计算开销的双屏障CBF安全滤波器，并采用自适应增益策略优化探索效率与安全性的平衡；该滤波器工作于速度空间，以最小干预方式与任意名义控制器结合。

Result: 所提方法仅需每周期求解一个小规模线性系统，在树莓派等资源受限平台上具有低计算开销，能够有效保留计算资源用于SLAM和规划任务；自适应增益策略提升了信息丰富区域的探索灵活性和已知区域的安全性；在PX4控制的四旋翼无人机上进行了实物飞行实验，多次室内运行均实现零碰撞。

Conclusion: 该双屏障CBF安全滤波器能够在存在感知不确定性的动态未知环境中，为移动机器人提供高效且可靠的安全保障，适用于资源受限平台，并兼容包括学习型控制器在内的各类控制方法。

Abstract: We present a dual-barrier control barrier function (CBF) safety filter for real-time, safety-critical velocity control of holonomic robots operating in incrementally built occupancy grid maps. As a robot explores an unknown environment, unmapped regions introduce irreducible uncertainty, since obstacle geometry beyond the explored frontier is unknown, making entry into such regions a source of collision risk, especially with front-facing sensors. To address this, we enforce two constraints: avoidance of mapped obstacles and restriction from unexplored regions. Both constraints are derived analytically from the occupancy grid's signed distance field, yielding a closed-form safety filter that requires only a small linear system solve per cycle. On resource-constrained platforms such as the Raspberry Pi, where SLAM and planning already consume significant compute, the low overhead of the proposed filter preserves resources. An adaptive gain schedule relaxes the frontier constraint in information-rich regions and tightens it in well-mapped areas, improving exploration efficiency while maintaining safety. The filter operates in velocity space as a minimally invasive correction and composes with arbitrary nominal controllers, including learning-based methods. Hardware flight experiments on a PX4-controlled quadrotor demonstrate zero collisions across multiple indoor runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Regularized Centered Emphatic Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04100)
*Xingguo Chen,Chaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Tianyu Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种正则化强调时差学习方法（RETD），通过在辅助中心递归中引入正则化来解决离策略时差学习中的稳定性、投影几何和方差控制之间的权衡问题，实验证明该方法在诊断性线性任务中表现稳定且有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略时差学习在使用函数逼近时面临稳定性、投影几何和方差控制之间的结构性权衡，现有方法如ETD虽然改进了投影几何但可能带来高方差问题，需要一种更稳健的解决方案。

Method: 通过贝尔曼误差中心化重新审视该权衡，并提出正则化强调时差学习（RETD），保留后续迹并对辅助中心递归进行正则化，相当于提升耦合关键矩阵右下角块的值。推导了RETD核心矩阵并在保守正则化条件下证明其收敛性。

Result: 实验表明RETD避免了朴素中心化强调学习的不稳定性，保持了良好的强调几何特性，并在不同诊断任务中对正则化参数c表现出鲁棒的中间区域。

Conclusion: RETD有效平衡了离策略预测中稳定性、几何特性和方差控制的需求，为离策略强化学习提供了一个更可靠的学习框架。

Abstract: Off-policy temporal-difference (TD) learning with function approximation faces a structural tradeoff among stability, projection geometry, and variance control. Emphatic TD (ETD) improves the off-policy projection geometry through follow-on emphasis, but the follow-on trace can have high variance. We revisit this tradeoff through Bellman-error centering. Although centering naturally removes a common drift term from TD errors, we show that a naive centered emphatic extension introduces an auxiliary coupling that can destroy the positive-definiteness of the ETD key matrix. We propose \emph{Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning} (RETD), which preserves the follow-on trace and regularizes only the auxiliary centering recursion, corresponding to lifting the lower-right block of the coupled key matrix from \(1\) to \(1+c\). We derive the RETD core matrix, prove convergence under a conservative sufficient regularization condition, and evaluate the method on diagnostic linear off-policy prediction tasks. The experiments show that RETD avoids the instability of naive centered emphatic learning, preserves favorable emphatic geometry, and exhibits a robust intermediate regime for the regularization parameter \(c\) across the diagnostics.

</details>


### [150] [ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193)
*Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的神经符号可微规则提取框架ANDRE，用于在连续规则空间中学习一阶逻辑程序，能够在不确定性和噪声条件下实现准确、稳定且可解释的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归纳逻辑编程（ILP）方法在处理噪声和概率性数据时存在扩展性差、对不确定性敏感、依赖预定义规则模板或使用不准确的模糊算子等问题，难以实现在复杂现实场景下的有效和可解释推理。

Method: 提出ANDRE框架，使用基于注意力机制的可微合取与析取算子替代传统规则模板和逻辑算子，逼近逻辑上的min-max语义，并通过软选择、否定或排除谓词实现灵活的规则归纳，支持在连续空间中优化一阶逻辑规则。

Result: 在经典ILP基准、大规模知识库和含噪声监督的合成数据集上实验表明，ANDRE在预测性能上具有竞争力或更优，能可靠地恢复正确的符号规则，且在中等标签噪声下显著优于现有可微ILP方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: ANDRE通过引入注意力驱动的可微逻辑算子，有效解决了传统和神经符号ILP方法在噪声和概率环境下的局限性，实现了高效、稳定且可解释的规则学习，为不确定条件下的归纳逻辑推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) aims to learn interpretable first-order rules from data, but existing symbolic and neuro-symbolic approaches struggle to scale to noisy and probabilistic settings. Classical ILP relies on discrete combinatorial rule search and is brittle under uncertainty, while differentiable ILP methods typically depend on predefined rule templates or inaccurate fuzzy operators that suffer from vanishing gradients or poor approximation of logical structure when reasoning over probabilistic predicate valuations. This paper proposes an Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor (ANDRE), a novel ILP framework that learns first-order logic programs by optimizing over a continuous rule space with attention-based logical operators. ANDRE replaces both rule templates and logical operators with fully differentiable, attention-driven conjunction and disjunction operators that approximate logical min-max semantics, enabling accurate, stable, and interpretable reasoning over probabilistic data. By softly selecting, negating, or excluding predicates within each rule, ANDRE supports flexible rule induction while preserving symbolic structure. Extensive experiments on classical ILP benchmarks, large-scale knowledge bases, and synthetic datasets with probabilistic predicates and noisy supervision demonstrate that ANDRE achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while reliably recovering correct symbolic rules under uncertainty. In particular, ANDRE remains robust to moderate label noise, substantially outperforming existing differentiable ILP methods in both rule extraction quality and stability.

</details>


### [151] [Pro$^2$Assist: Continuous Step-Aware Proactive Assistance with Multimodal Egocentric Perception for Long-Horizon Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04227)
*Lilin Xu,Bufang Yang,Siyang Jiang,Kaiwei Liu,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Hongkai Chen,Zhenyu Yan,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Pro^2Assist的步态感知主动式助手，利用增强现实（AR）眼镜的多模态数据，实现对长时间过程性任务的连续跟踪与主动辅助，显著提升了任务理解与干预时机的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个人助手系统主要依赖用户查询提供被动指导，或仅支持短期孤立事件的有限主动辅助，难以应对日常生活中复杂的、多步骤的过程性任务。

Method: Pro^2Assist利用来自AR眼镜的多模态数据进行基于动作的感知，从多尺度时间动态和任务特定专家知识中提取以步骤为导向的程序上下文，并结合感官输入与上下文信息进行持续推理，以推断用户需求并在AR眼镜上提供及时辅助。

Result: 在公开数据集和自建AR测试平台真实数据集上的实验表明，Pro^2Assist在程序动作理解准确率上比最佳基线高出21%以上，主动干预时机准确率最高达到基线的2.29倍；20名参与者的用户研究表明90%的用户认为其有用。

Conclusion: Pro^2Assist能够有效支持长时间、多步骤的日常任务，通过细粒度的任务进度追踪与持续推理，显著提升了主动辅助系统的实用性与准确性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks with multiple ordered steps are ubiquitous in daily life. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled personal assistants that support daily activities. However, existing systems primarily provide reactive guidance triggered by user queries, or limited proactive assistance for isolated short-term events rather than long-horizon procedural tasks. In this work, we introduce Pro$^2$Assist, a step-aware proactive assistant that continuously tracks fine-grained task progress and reasons over the user's evolving state to provide timely assistance throughout tasks. Pro$^2$Assist leverages multimodal data from augmented reality (AR) glasses to achieve motion-based perception. It then extracts step-oriented procedural context from multi-scale temporal dynamics and task-specific expert knowledge. Based on both sensory input and procedural context, Pro$^2$Assist performs continuous reasoning to infer user needs and display timely assistance on AR glasses. We evaluate Pro$^2$Assist using a dataset curated from public sources and a real-world dataset collected on our testbed with AR glasses. Extensive evaluations show that Pro$^2$Assist outperforms the best-performing baselines by over 21% in procedural action understanding accuracy, and it achieves up to 2.29x the proactive timing accuracy of baselines. A user study with 20 participants further shows that 90% find Pro$^2$Assist useful, indicating its effectiveness for real-world procedural assistance.

</details>


### [152] [Temporal Reasoning Is Not the Bottleneck: A Probabilistic Inconsistency Framework for Neuro-Symbolic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04243)
*Tran Quang Liem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的神经符号问答框架，通过概率不一致性信号（PIS）将文本到事件的表示错误与推理失败分离，实验证明时间推理性能瓶颈主要在于结构化表示而非推理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型取得进展，但在复杂的时间推理任务上仍表现脆弱，通常归因于自回归逻辑推理缺陷；本文挑战这一观点，认为根本问题在于非结构化文本到事件表示的不准确。

Method: 提出一个由PIS驱动的神经符号框架，将非结构化文本映射为显式的事件图和区间约束，从而分离语义提取与符号推理；PIS结合符号可信区间与基于LLM隐藏状态的证据深度学习所提取的认知不确定性，用于检测结构断裂。

Result: 在提供正确结构表示时，系统在时间算术基准上达到1.0准确率（4000/4000），零误报/漏报；在噪声注入的问答场景中保持75.1%准确率，并支持确定性的逐级故障定位。

Conclusion: 时间问答的主要瓶颈是文本到结构的对齐问题，而非推理能力不足；通过解耦表示与推理，本文为可靠神经符号AI提供了可验证的发展路径。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to exhibit brittle performance on complex temporal reasoning tasks. This failure mode is widely attributed to inherent deficits in autoregressive logical deduction.
